All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. Please review the terms and conditions of your bank or contact them directly to confirm whether additional fees may apply when using Zelle.\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCARD PAYMENTS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nVisa, American Express, MasterCard and Discover utilized for registration will be accepted as a form of payment for purchases under $500.00-please see above for buyer's premium with convenience fee information on this auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nNo automatic payments are processed. To use a credit card, buyer must contact auctioneer to authorize charge.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eWIRE TRANSFERS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll wire transfers must be received by the auctioneers no later than the stated time outlined above. Be aware wire transfers can take 24 hours to process and is buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn AI assisted software was used to write portions of the Details of the Auction Info.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e13% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases for cash, wire transfer, Zelle, cashier's check.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n15% buyer's premium along with convenience fee for credit card-Visa, MasterCard, Discover, American Express.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e7.50% sales tax\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n1501 West Wesley Avenue\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDenver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\nTelephone: (303) 934-8322\u003cbr\u003e\r\nToll Free: (877) 284-0338\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n \u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e \u003c/p\u003e",

    "formatted_simple_description": "\u003cp\u003eLAKEWOOD - ESTATE - Royal Albert, Lenox China and More\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSTAGGERED ONLINE BIDDING ENDS: Tuesday, May 12th 2:00 PM MT\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eREMOVAL: Wednesday, May 13th 9:00 AM - 4:00 PM MT\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eADDRESS: Green Gables Community, Lakewood, CO 80227\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e13% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases for cash, wire transfer, Zelle, cashier's check.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e15% buyer's premium for credit card and convenience fee-Visa, MasterCard, Discover, American Express.\u003c/p\u003e",

    "terms": {
        "id": 69684,
        "name": "2025 AUCTION TERMS \u0026 CONDITIONS (13%BP)",
        "company_id": 492,
        "legalese": "\u003cp\u003eTERMS AND CONDITIONS FOR THIS AUCTION\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eGeneral Terms and Conditions\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cspan style=\"font-size:12pt;\"\u003e\u003cspan style='font-family:\"Aptos\",sans-serif;'\u003e\u003cspan style=\"font-size:11.0pt;\"\u003eBy registering for this auction, the registrant acknowledges and agrees to all terms and conditions outlined below. This includes full understanding and adherence to any line items listed prior to Lot 1 in the catalog, as well as the information presented in the Details Section.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAll items are offered for sale \"AS-IS, WHERE-IS\". Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. makes no warranty, guarantee or representation of any kind, expressed or implied, as to the merchantability or fitness for any purpose of the property offered for sale. The Buyer is not entitled to any payment for loss of profit or any other money damages-special, direct, indirect or consequential. Any and all disputes or concerns MUST be addressed prior to the removal of the lot(s) prior to the auction site.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll parties must be over the age of 18 to participate with the auction or be present in the impound yard before, during or after removal.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nBidder is required to provide true, accurate, current and complete information and maintain, promptly update the registration data to keep it true, accurate, current and complete. Please note that the registration address will need to match the address associated with the card utilized during the process to create an account.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nBuyer is responsible for all transactions on its account, including without limitation, all bids placed by authorized bidders and bid submitted through the Auctioneer’s bidding platform. Accounts cannot be transferred. In the event of unauthorized use of Buyer’s login information, Buyer is responsible for all bids.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThe Auctioneer is acting as agent only and is not responsible for actions of its Principals. The Auctioneer shall assume no liability for representations made, or information provided by the Seller.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nBy registration, the registered party is authorizing Auctioneer communication via email, landline, or cell/mobile phone including text messaging and/or any other forms of communication or technology now known or created in the future, for any purpose, including general business matters, account information, marketing materials, collections, and/or any other communication needs.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAuctioneer has made every reasonable effort to facilitate online bidding. However, it is possible that technical difficulties and internet issues may arise that affect Auction Company’s website, bidding platform, computer, server or the Bidder’s computer, personal device or software which is beyond the control of Auction Company. By registering, Bidder acknowledges that the Auctioneer is not responsible for any technical difficulties or internet issues associated with a bid and that, in the event technical difficulties interfere with or prevent a bid, it may be necessary to withdraw items from bidding due to technical errors or uncontrollable circumstances. Further, in the event of the occurrence of a technical difficulty during an Auction, Auction Company, in its sole discretion, reserve the right to: (1) declare a Property sold, (2) postpone or cancel the Auction, (3) extend the bidding time for the Auction (which may reopen bidding on closed items, if any) and/or (4) re-list the Property for auction at another date and time. The decision on this matter shall be final. Bidder acknowledges that, by registering for ay auction held by Auction Company, Bidder accepts the terms and conditions of this disclaimer.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThis staggered bidding online auction is subject to soft close. If a bid is received shortly before the lot closes, the system will automatically extend the bidding time to allow other bidders an opportunity to competitively bid prior to the auction closing. The method eliminates “snipers” and encourages fair and impartial bidding from all participants.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nBy law, all auctions are with reserve unless specifically stated to be Absolute per UCC 2-328 and applicable state law.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll parties enter the lot at their own risk. Neither Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, inc. nor the Seller(s), Landlord is responsible for personal injury or lost items regarding this auction including inspection (if applicable) and removal.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPersons removing the goods assume all risks of damage or loss to person and property and specifically release Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc., Seller(s), Landlord from liability. Therefore, neither Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc, Seller(s), Landlord, nor its principal(s), shall be liable by reason of any defect in, or condition of premises on which auction is held.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDefault shall include: (1) failure to observe these terms \u0026amp; conditions; (2) failure to make good and timely payment; or (3) failure to remove all items within the specified time. Default will result in the suspension from participation in all future sales. If the Buyer fails in the performance of their obligations, Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. may exercise such rights and may pursue such remedies as are provided by law.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nBy submitting a bid, the bidder agrees that they have read, fully understand and accept these Terms \u0026amp; Conditions of Online Sales, and agree to pay for and remove the property, if the bid is accepted, by the dates and times specified.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAYMENT TERMS AND CONDITIONS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003ePLEASE REVIEW THE DETAILS SECTION FOR THE TYPE OF PAYMENT BEING ACCEPTED AT THIS PARTICULAR AUCTION.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cspan style=\"font-size:12pt;\"\u003e\u003cspan style='font-family:\"Aptos\",sans-serif;'\u003eThe purchaser is responsible for ensuring that full payment is received by the Auctioneer within the designated timeframe.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eTHE FOLLOWING REPRESENTS THE VARIETY OF ACCEPTABLE PAYMENTS FOR THE VARIETY OF AUCTIONS CONDUCTED BY AUCTIONEER:\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nWIRE TRANSFERS-All wire transfers would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to removal stated time period.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nCASH-VECTRA BANK-Cash transactions can be accepted at Vectra Bank locations throughout Colorado following the auction. Please call 303-934-8322 for further information on completion.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nCASH-WESLEY LOCATION-Cash transactions can also be accepted at Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc., 1501 W Wesley Avenue, Denver, CO 80223.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPREAPPROVED BUSINESS/PERSONAL CHECKS-Please call 303-934-8322 prior to participating with the auction for funds verification process.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nCREDIT CARD-Visa, MasterCard, Discover or American Express with 2% convenience fee.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n13% BUYERS PREMIUM FOR CASH OR CASH EQUIVALENT PAYMENTS.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eThese terms and conditions provide clarity and ensure that all participants are well-informed about the auction process and their responsibilities.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e",
